                laid down under these Regulations; or

                                                                                     8
BSE Limited  Capital Market Segment - Regulations

          iii) on receipt of a request to that effect from the member or the Authorised
                  Person, subject however to compliance with the requirements prescribed by the
                  Exchange from time to time.

          iv) Without prejudice to the above, the Exchange may take disciplinary action
                  against the Authorised Person under the provisions of Rules, Bye-Laws, circulars,
                  directions of the Exchange and also refer/ report the matter to SEBI for
                  appropriate action.

2.2.4.11 Notwithstanding anything contained in Regulations 2.2.4.10, an Authorised Person
          shall cease to be so, ipso facto, on

          (a) the member, to whom he is attached, ceasing to be the member; or

          (b) on the expiry / cessation of his agreement /contract with such member.

2.2.4.12 Any dispute between a Constituent and an Authorised Person shall be treated as a dispute
          between the Constituent and the member and the Constituent shall be entitled to take
          out the proceedings against the member with whom such Authorised Person was
          attached/associated.

2.2.4.13 Whenever an Approved User of a member ceases to act either as an Approved User
          or in any other capacity with such member then such member shall inform the
          Exchange, within 24 hours, the name and other particulars of such Approved User.

2.2.4.14 The Exchange may notify different types of Approved Users for different workstations. The
          access that the Approved User shall have to the Trading System will depend on the
          category in which such Approved User falls and depending thereon such Approved User
          will either be entitled to only place his inquiry on the terminal, or to place the order entry
          and trade or to so such others matters on the Trading System as may be specified by the
          Exchange.

2.2.4.15 A member/ Approved User shall not access the Trading System using a different User
          Id other than the one allotted to him.

2.2.4.16 An Approved User shall not access or attempt to or aid in accessing the Trading System
          using the member's code from a location other than the member's location, unless he
          has the express prior approval of the member for whom he is acting as an Approved
          User.

2.3    REMISIERS
2.3.1
       A person who is engaged by a member primarily to solicit commission business in
       securities shall be called a remisier; provided engagement of such person is approved
       by the Relevant Authority.

2.3.2 Commission Terms

          A member shall be entitled to employ remisiers for the purpose of his stock exchange
          business, and subject to the provisions of the Bye Laws, remunerate such remisers with
          such brokerage as agreed upon in writing by way of an agreement and pursuant to the

                                                                                                                            9
BSE Limited                                            Capital Market Segment - Regulations

       approval received from the Relevant Authority.

2.3.3 Permission Necessary

          No member shall employ any remisier without first having such appointment
          approved by the Relevant Authority.

2.3.4 Ex-members as Remisiers

          A member may, with the special consent of the Relevant Authority employ as his
          remisier a person who has ceased to be a member under the Rules, Bye-laws and
          Regulations of the Exchange.

2.3.5  Remisier Not to be another's Employee

       A remisier may be an individual or firm but must not be in the employment of any
       individual or firm.

2.3.6  Remisier to Act for one member only
2.3.7
       No person shall be registered as a remisier to more than one member.

       Application

       a) A member desirous of employing a remisier shall apply for the permission of the
              Relevant Authority in such form as the Relevant Authority may from time to time
              prescribe. Such form shall be signed both by the member and the proposed
              remisier.

              Discharge Certificate

       b) An application by a member to employ a remisier who previously had been
              acting as a remisier with another member must be accompanied by a discharge
              certificate from the former employer or employers. Such discharge certificate
              shall be in such form as the Relevant Authority may from time to time prescribe
              and it shall show whether the remisier left his former employer or employers
              clear of all debts and outstanding liabilities and whether his conduct while in
              that employment was satisfactory.

              Absence of Discharge Certificate
       c) When a discharge certificate is not attached to an application as required under

              sub-clause (b) the proposed remisier shall submit an explanation therefor and the
              Relevant Authority shall then decide whether and on what conditions the

                                                                                                                     10
BSE Limited  Capital Market Segment - Regulations

             requirements relating to the discharge certificate shall be waived and if there
             be any dispute between the proposed remisier and his former employer the
             Relevant Authority may refer it for adjudication by arbitration in accordance
             with the Bye-laws and Regulations of the Exchange relating to arbitration other
             than between members.

2.3.8   Remisier Not to Engage in Forward Business

        The Relevant Authority may refuse permission to a person desirous of working as a
        remisier unless he gives an undertaking that forthwith on registration as remisier he
        will cease to engage himself as principal or employee in and not be directly or
        indirectly connected with any other kind of forward business.

2.3.9   Approval or Rejection of Application

        The Relevant Authority in its discretion may approve or reject any application for
        appointment of a remisier.

2.3.10  Withdrawal/Suspension of Permission

        The Relevant Authority in its discretion may at any time terminate/suspend the
        approval granted to a remisier for violation of the Rules, Bye-Laws, Regulations, terms
        and requirements of the Exchange as prescribed from time to time or conducting itself
        in a disorderly manner.

2.3.11  Termination

        On termination of the approval, the remisier shall discontinue to be the remisier of
        the member of the Exchange.

2.3.12  Register of Remisiers

        (a) A register of remisiers shall be maintained by the Exchange in which shall be
               entered the names of all remisiers together with the dates of their appointment
               and discharge and the names of the members employing them.

        Registration and Removal

        (b) The Relevant Authority shall have full power to refuse registration or to remove
               the name of any remisier from the register without assigning any reason.

             11
BSE Limited                       Capital Market Segment - Regulations

2.3.13 Remisier Not to Make Deal

        A remisier shall not make deal in his own name or on behalf of his employer or any other
        member on the trading platform provided by the Exchange. A remisier acting in violation
        of this provision shall be immediately suspended or expelled by the Relevant Authority.

2.3.14  Remisier Not to Sign Contract Notes

        A remisier shall give the names of his constituents in whose names contract notes are to
        be rendered by the member for whom the remisier acts. The remisier shall not sign
        contract notes in his own or any other name nor shall he sign on behalf of his employer
        unless appointed by such employer as his constituted attorney for that purpose.

2.3.15  Remisier's Personal Business

        A remisier may not act as a remisier for his personal business. No allowance shall be
        made for the brokerage upon business for his own personal account.

2.3.16  Sub-Agent

        If a remisier in whatever circumstances directly or indirectly divides or shares his
        brokerage with his constituent or sub-agent, the permission granted by the
        Exchange shall be withdrawn.

2.3.17  Remisier Not to Share Brokerage with Constituents

        Members shall be responsible to ensure that remisiers make no allowance or return
        of brokerage directly or indirectly to the constituents they introduce or to any other
        person or agent.

2.3.18  Security

        (a) When the employing member so requires a remisier shall on registration provide
             security for the sum of at least Rs. 5,000 and shall maintain such security with the
             Exchange at all times during the period he continues to be a remisier with such
             member.

             Provisions as to Security
        (b) The provisions in the Bye-laws and these Regulations relating to security

             provided by a member shall apply mutatis mutandis to security provided by a
             remisier under sub-clause (a).

                                                                                                                    12
BSE Limited  Capital Market Segment - Regulations

              Lien on Security

       (c) The member for whom a remisier is working shall have a first charge on the security
              for the due fulfilment of his engagements, obligations or liabilities arising out of or
              incidental to any deal, dealings, transactions and contracts made with him or on his
              behalf or with or on behalf of his constituents subject to the Rules, Bye-laws and
              Regulations of the Exchange or anything done in pursuance thereof.

              Return of Security

       (d) On the termination of his employment or in the event of his death all security
              not applied under the Rules, Bye-laws and Regulations of the Exchange shall at
              the cost of the remisier be repaid and transferred to him or as he shall direct or
              in the absence of such direction to his legal representatives.

2.4    TRADING DAYS
2.4.1
       The Exchange will operate on all such days as specified by the Exchange from time to
       time.

2.4.2  The Exchange may close the market on days other than or in addition to scheduled
       holidays or open the market on days originally declared to be holidays under 2.4.1 and
       the decision of the Exchange in this matter shall be final and binding.

2.5    TRADING HOURS
2.5.1
       The Exchange shall announce the regular trading hours for every calendar year in
2.5.2  advance, segment wise.

       The Exchange may extend, advance or reduce trading hours by notifying the members
       as and when it deems fit and necessary in this regard or as may be directed by the
       Relevant Authority from time to time in this regard.

2.6    TRADING PARAMETERS
2.6.1
2.6.2  The Exchange may from time to time specify various trading parameters relating to
       the Trading System.
2.6.3
2.6.4  The Exchange from time to time may specify one or more order books that shall be
2.6.5  maintained on the Trading System and shall specify such various conditions that shall
       make the order eligible to place it in those books.

       The Exchange shall specify the minimum disclosed quantity for orders that will be
       allowed.

       The Exchange shall specify from time to time the lot size in which orders can be placed
       for any or all securities traded on the Exchange.

       The Exchange may specify from time to time price steps in which orders shall be entered
                                                                                                                     13
BSE Limited  Capital Market Segment - Regulations

2.6.6  on the Trading System of the Exchange.
2.6.7
       The Exchange shall specify the maximum and/or minimum number of days for which
       Repo trades will be allowed by the Exchange, in the Wholesale Debt Market Segment.

       The Exchange will prescribe position limits / price limits for various types of deals, as
       deemed fit and/or directed by SEBI in respect of Electronic Gold Receipts available for
       trading in the EGR Segment and the method for clubbing the open positions from time
       to time.

2.7    TRADE TYPES/SETTLEMENT PERIOD/TRANSACTION TYPES
2.7.1
2.7.2  Unless otherwise provided by the Exchange, the securities in which the trades are
2.7.3  carried out shall be deemed to be included in the official list.

2.7.4  The Exchange shall prescribe from time to time different trade types, market types
       that will be permitted to the members for dealings in securities.

       The Clearing Corporation shall prescribe from time to time different settlement cycles
       / periods that will be permitted to the members or securities and the same will be in
       consultation with the Exchange, if required.

       The Exchange shall allow from time to time different Transaction Types which shall
       include transactions for clearing for hand delivery, for special delivery or for spot
       delivery.

2.8 FAILURE OF MEMBERS' TERMINAL

          In the event of failure of the members' workstations and/or the loss of access to the
          Trading System, the Exchange may, upon receipt of a valid written request (in the
          manner prescribed by the Exchange) from the member, entirely at its sole discretion,
          undertake such action on behalf of the member, subject to the availability of the
          necessary infrastructure. Such action shall be undertaken by the Exchange on such
          terms and conditions which the Exchange may deem necessary to be imposed. The
          member shall be solely responsible for the action undertaken by the Exchange on his/its
          behalf as if such action had been undertaken by the member himself, and shall
          indemnify the Exchange against any loss, charges, expenses, damages or costs arising
          out of any such action undertaken by the Exchange.

             14
BSE Limited  Capital Market Segment - Regulations

             CHAPTER 3 - DEALINGS IN SECURITIES

3.1     DEALINGS IN SECURITIES
3.1.1
        Dealings in securities shall be permitted on the Exchange in accordance with the
        provisions of the Rules, Bye-laws and Regulations of the Exchange for such categories
        of members, trade types, market types, and for such trading hours as the Exchange
        may specify from time to time.

3.1.1A  The Exchange may offer its platform(s) for buying/selling/ subscribing/
        redeeming/transacting in various types of units, products or securities other than
        those specifically covered in Regulation 3.1.1 above, in accordance with the
        framework prescribed by regulators, in such manner and on such terms and
        conditions as the Exchange may specify from time to time.

        Subject to the framework prescribed by the regulators and in accordance with their
        directions, the Exchange may, from time to time specify the provisions of the extant
        Rules, Bye-laws and the Regulations of the Exchange which shall apply to dealing in
        such units, products and securities offered by the Exchange through its platform(s).

3.1.2 In case of corporate member:

           (a) The Articles of Association of a Trading Member which is a company shall
                  contain such provisions as the Relevant Authority may from time to time
                  require. A list of shareholders of the company shall be furnished to the
                  Exchange. The shareholders for the time being shall be persons acceptable to
                  the Relevant Authority;

           (b) The name, Memorandum and Articles of Association, capital and shareholding
                  pattern of such company shall not be altered without the prior consent of the
                  Relevant Authority;

           (c) A person shall not be appointed as a director of such company unless the
                  Relevant Authority conveys its approval to such appointment;

        (d) In the case of a company formed either under the Companies Act, 1956 or under
              the Companies Act, 2013, the company shall declare its dominant shareholders at
              the time of seeking admission to the membership of the Exchange and shall appoint
              at least one of the dominant shareholders as one of its Designated Directors.

              If, however, it is not feasible for a company to appoint one of its dominant
              shareholders as a Designated Director, then the company shall appoint at least
              two Designated Directors, who should possess:

                                                                                                                     15
BSE Limited  Capital Market Segment - Regulations

             (a) educational qualification of not less than graduation; and

             (b) minimum of two years' experience in capital markets.
             (c) should not be a designated director of another Trading Member of the

                   Exchange.

3.1.3   Provided further that the Relevant Authority has the power to waive the above
3.1.4   requirements for a company (including a company promoted by Non-Resident Indians)
        on such conditions as it finds fit subject to the provisions of Securities Laws. The Relevant
        Authority has the power to define the concept of Dominant Shareholder.

        The member(s) may trade on the Trading System in securities that are admitted for
        dealing on the Exchange or permitted securities, either on behalf of their Constituents
        or on their own account unless otherwise specified by the Relevant Authority and
        trading shall be subject to such conditions as the Exchange may prescribe from time
        to time at its sole discretion.

        At the time of entering an order on behalf of a Mutual Fund or any of its Schemes,
        Foreign Institutional Investor/Foreign Portfolio Investor or any of its sub-account holders
        or any other category of investors as may be specified by SEBI and/or BSE, the member
        shall enter the unique code in respect of such Mutual Fund or its Scheme, Foreign
        Institutional Investors or any of its sub-accounts or any other category of investor in the
        manner and as may be specified by SEBI and/or BSE from time to time.

3.1.5   The Exchange may, at any time restrict conditionally or unconditionally a member
3.1.6   from dealing in a specified security or securities.
3.1.7
3.1.8   A member shall continue to be liable for all trades executed on the system, for orders
         entered into the Trading System by him or on his behalf. A member shall be
3.1.9    responsible for all the actions of its Authorised Persons/Approved Users.

3.1.10   Without prejudice to Regulation 4.6.3 (c) a member shall be responsible for all the
         actions, including trades originating through or with the use of various variables
         including a) member Id, b) User Id, c) valid User password at that point of time.

         When a member enters an order on behalf of a Constituent, the member shall, enter
         the unique code allotted to such Constituent in such manner as may be notified by the
         Exchange. Every member shall be responsible to furnish particulars of unique client codes
         of each of his Constituents to the Exchange in such form, manner, at such intervals and
         within such time as may be specified by the Exchange from time to time.

        A member shall, in respect of all transactions in securities, where the total quantity of
         shares bought/sold under proprietary or any single client code is more than such
         percentage of the number of equity shares of the company listed / permitted to be traded
         on the Exchange as may be prescribed by the Relevant Authority from time to time,
         disclose to the Exchange immediately upon execution of the trade, the name of the scrip,
         name of the Constituent, quantity of shares bought / sold and the traded price.

        The information mentioned in Regulation 3.1.8 shall be furnished to the Exchange in
         such format and within such time as maybe prescribed by the Exchange, from time to
         time.

                                                                                                                       16
BSE Limited  Capital Market Segment - Regulations

3.1A LISTING AGREEMENT

           (a) The Company shall execute or undertake to execute under its seal a Listing
                  Agreement in the form prescribed by the SEBI/the Exchange from time to time.

        (b) The Company incorporated outside India, shall execute or undertake to execute
              under its seal a Listing Agreement (Listing Agreement for IDRs) in such form as
              may be prescribed by SEBI/the Exchange from to time.

3.1A.1  Listing of New Capital Issues

        Companies which are listed on the Exchange shall apply for admission to dealings on
        the Exchange of any new (original or further) issue of securities made by them in the
        manner prescribed by the Exchange.

3.1A.2  Supporting Documents

        Companies applying for admission of their securities to dealings on the Exchange shall
        submit to the Exchange the documents and information as mentioned in various
        forms/checklist/circulars or such other additional documents and information as the
        Exchange may require from time to time.

3.1A.3  Listing Conditions and Requirements

        All Companies which are listed on the Exchange shall comply with the listing
        conditions and requirements contained in the relevant listing agreement, Rules, Bye-
        laws and Regulations, securities laws and regulations prescribed by SEBI, and such
        other conditions as may be prescribed by the Exchange.

3.1A.4  The Exchange may, at its discretion, at any time suspend trading in a security or
        securities as it deems fit. Such suspension shall take effect, on such conditions and in
        such time and manner as the Exchange may prescribe in this regard specifically or
        generally.

3.1A.5  Without prejudice to the rights of the Exchange to suspend trading in any security, in
        the event of non-compliance with any conditions of the Listing Agreement and
        Regulations prescribed by SEBI, the Exchange may take such other actions, including
        levying a penalty, as may be provided or directed by SEBI or as may be decided by the
        Exchange, from time to time.

3.1A.6  The Exchange may also revoke the suspension of trading in securities at any time as may
        be directed/provided by the SEBI or as the Exchange may deem fit, in the interest

                                                                                                                      17
BSE Limited  Capital Market Segment - Regulations

        of the investors of the securities market from time to time.

3.1A.7  The Exchange shall, for the purpose of revocation of suspension of trading in securities,
        prescribe fees from time to time, which shall be binding on the companies and the
        companies shall be required to pay the same for revocation of suspension.

3.2     TRADE OPERATIONS
3.2.1
        The members shall ensure that appropriate confirmed order instructions are obtained
        from the Constituents before placement of an order on the system and shall keep
        relevant records or documents of the same and of the completion or otherwise of
        these orders.

3.2.2   The member shall make available to his Constituent the Trading System order number
        and shall dispatch copies of the order confirmation slips / modification slips.

3.2.3   The procedure and conditions for amendment or cancellation of orders would be as
        specified by the Exchange from time to time.

3.2.4   The members shall be solely responsible for the accuracy of details of all orders
        entered into the Trading System including orders entered on behalf of his
        Constituents.

3.2.5   Orders entered into the Trading System shall be subject to various validation
        requirements as specified by the Exchange from time to time including but not limited
        to trading parameters, turnover limits, exposure limits and/or other restrictions
        placed on traded securities. Orders that do not meet the validation checks shall not
        be accepted by the Trading System. If however such orders are accepted by the
        Trading System due to any reason whatsoever, the member shall be liable and
        responsible to honour the same or comply with such other directions as may be given
        by the Exchange.

3.2.6   Trades generated on the Trading System are irrevocable and 'locked in'. However, the
        Exchange may annul/cancel the trades in accordance with the provisions of Rules,
        Bye-laws and Regulations.

3.2.7 (a) Without prejudice to the decision taken with regards to annulment of trades(s),
                  the Relevant Authority shall also be entitled to take such other suitable
                  disciplinary action(s) against the members under the Rules, Bye-Laws and
                  Regulations of the Exchange.

                                                                                                                          18
BSE Limited  Capital Market Segment - Regulations

       (b) The procedure to be followed for annulment of trades(s) shall be such as
              specified by the Exchange from time to time and every member shall comply
              with the same including payment of prescribed fees.

3.3    MARGIN REQUIREMENTS
3.3.1
       Subject to the provisions as contained in the Rules, Bye-laws and Regulations, every
3.3.2  member shall, in respect of trades, which he proposes to execute/has executed, to
       which he is a party, deposit margins with the Exchange, in such manner, to the extent
       and from such date as may be specified by the Exchange.

       The Exchange shall prescribe from time to time the securities, the settlement periods
       and trade types for which margins would be attracted.

3.3.3  The margins shall be deposited with the Exchange within such time as may be notified
       by the Exchange from time to time.

3.3.4  The Exchange shall prescribe from time to time such categories of securities that
       would be eligible as a margin deposit as also the method of valuation and amount of
       securities that would be required to be so deposited against the margin amount.

3.3.5 In the event, a member applies for surrender of his membership, the deposits/margin
          of such member lying with the Exchange shall be retained/dealt with in accordance with
          circulars/directions issued by SEBI/the Exchange from time to time.

          The Exchange will return/refund the amount(s)/deposits of such member after
          adjusting the same against any outstanding obligations/liability of such member, to
          the member or his/her heirs or legal representatives in accordance with the provisions
          contained in the Rules, Bye-laws and Regulations of the Exchange.

3.4    COMPOSITION OF ADDITIONAL CAPITAL AND MARGINS

       The Relevant Authority may specify the requirements of additional capital and
       margins for the members and the liquid assets that may be placed by them for
       meeting such requirements. The minimum cash component of such additional capital
       and margins shall be as specified by the Relevant Authority from time to time and the
       cash component may be in the form of cash or cash equivalents in the form and
       manner and subject to such conditions and restrictions as may be specified by the
       Relevant Authority from time to time. The non-cash component shall be placed in such
       form and manner as may be specified by the Relevant Authority from time to time.

                                                      19
BSE Limited  Capital Market Segment - Regulations

3.5    ORDER MANAGEMENT
3.5.1
       ORDER TYPE

       The Exchange shall stipulate from time to time, the kinds of orders that a member
       can place in the Trading System, which may include Normal order, Special Term order,
       etc. as also the order attributes that he could place thereon.

3.5.2  ORDER ATTRIBUTES

       (a) The Exchange shall from time to time allow various order attributes subject to
             trading parameters prescribed by the Exchange.

       (b) The Exchange shall specify the order types and order attributes permitted for
             different market types, trade types, etc.

3.5.3 MODIFICATION AND CANCELLATION OF ORDERS

           (a) A member shall be permitted to modify or cancel his orders, provided a trade
                  has not already taken place in respect of that order.

           (b) The order can be modified by effecting changes in the order input parameters
                  in the manner and on such conditions as specified by the Exchange.

           (c) The modified order may not retain its time priority unless otherwise decided by
                  the Exchange.

3.5.4 MATCHING RULES
           (a) The Exchange shall specify from time to time the kinds of order books that shall
                  be maintained on the Trading System, the order matching algorithms and the
                  matching rules and parameters that shall be followed therein.

           (b) The Exchange may modify or change such matching algorithms or order books
                  any time where it is necessary to do so.

           (c) Where the Exchange feels that it is in the interests of the market to do so, it may
                  at any time make unavailable any particular order books or forms of matching,
                  in case of a particular security or a member or to the market as a whole.

           (d) Without prejudice to the generality of the above, the order matching rules
                  would include the following:

                  (i) Orders in the normal market shall be matched on price - time priority
                         basis.

                                                                                                                          20
BSE Limited  Capital Market Segment - Regulations

             (ii) The best buy order shall match with the best sell order. For trading on
                    price, the best buy order would be the one with the highest price and the
                    best sell order would be the one with the lowest price.

       (e) The Trading System shall store orders for the purpose of matching in different
              order books.

3.6    CONTRACT NOTE
3.6.1
       Every member shall issue a contract note to his Constituents for trades executed in
       such format as may be prescribed by the Exchange from time to time with all relevant
       details as required therein to be filled in, and issued in such manner and within such
       time as specified by the Exchange.

3.6.2  A contract note shall be signed by a member or his Authorised signatory or constituted
       Attorney.

3.6.3  The contract notes shall be numbered with unique running serial numbers
       commencing from one which shall be reset only at the beginning of every financial
       year.

3.6.4 Contract note shall be time stamped with the time of execution of order.

3.7 ELECTRONIC CONTRACT NOTE

          Notwithstanding anything contained in Regulation 3.6, a contract note may also be
          issued by a member in electronic form, in such format as may be prescribed by the
          Exchange from time to time, duly authenticated by means of a digital signature as
          specified in the Information Technology Act, 2000 and the Rules made thereunder,
          subject to any further conditions that may be specified by the Exchange/SEBI.

3.8 BROKERAGE

3.8.1 All the orders entered on the Trading System shall be at prices exclusive of brokerage.

3.8.2  The members shall charge brokerage but the same shall not exceed such scale as the
       Exchange/SEBI may specify from time to time.

3.8.3. The brokerage charged by a member shall be indicated separately from the price, in
                                                                                                                          21
BSE Limited                 Capital Market Segment - Regulations

        the contract note.

3.9 INTEREST, DIVIDEND, RIGHTS/BONUS AND CALLS

          The buyer shall be entitled to receive all coupons, dividends, bonus, rights and other
          privileges which may appertain to securities cum coupon, cum dividend, cum bonus,
          cum rights, etc. and the seller shall be entitled to receive all coupons, dividends, bonus
          issues, rights and other privileges which may appertain to securities sold ex coupon,
          ex dividend, ex-bonus, ex rights, etc.

3.10    NEGOTIATED TRADE MARKET
3.10.1
        The Exchange may permit members from time to time to enter trades on the
        Negotiated trade market. A negotiated trade or the orders underlying the trades
        shall be subject to all validation requirements as prescribed by the Exchange from
        time to time. Such trades will be subject to and shall comply with all provisions
        relating to all trades on the Exchange except where such provisions are specifically
        made inapplicable.

3.10.2  All negotiated trades entered into on the Trading System shall be subject to the
        approval of the Exchange. The Exchange shall have a right to approve or cancel a
        negotiated trade or throw open both the orders of the trade for Participation without
        giving any reasons thereof.

3.10.3  The Exchange may disallow specific securities, settlement types, transaction types,
        trade types, order types, members or participants for the purpose of effecting trades
        on the negotiated trade market.

3.11 MARGIN FROM THE CONSTITUENTS

          A member may demand from his Constituents a margin deposit which the member
          has to provide to the Exchange under the Rules, Bye-laws and Regulations in respect
          of the business done by the member for such Constituents. The members may buy
          securities on behalf of the Constituents only on the receipt of a minimum percentage
          of margin unless the Constituents already have equivalent credit with the members.
          The members may not, if he/ they so desire, collect such margins from the Financial
          Institutions, Mutual funds and Foreign Institutional Investors/Foreign Portfolio
          Investors.

        The members may sell securities on behalf of the Constituents only on the receipt of
        a minimum percentage of margins on the price of the securities proposed to be sold,
        unless the members have received the securities along with valid transfer documents
        in case of physical securities to their satisfaction prior to such sale. The members may
        not, if they so desire, collect such margins from the Financial Institutions, Mutual

                                                                                                                      22
BSE Limited                                  Capital Market Segment - Regulations

funds, and Foreign Institutional Investors.

The member may keep the un-utilised margin deposits of his client in bank deposits
and pay interest on the same at such rate as may be mutually agreed in writing
between the member and his constituent out of the interest accrued on the said
deposits.

3.12 MEMBER IN DEFAULT

           For the purpose of Bye-law 12 of Chapter XII of the Bye-laws of the Exchange, the
           period within which a Constituent shall close out his contract shall be seven trading
           days from the date of pay out of securities due to him; Provided however, if the
           constituent has not effected the close out as stated above, he shall be deemed to
           have closed out at the highest price of the said security deliverable to him from the
           date of trading till the 7th trading day from the date of pay out and loss or damage
           referred in the said Bye-law shall be ascertained accordingly.

3.13 CONSTITUENT IN DEFAULT

           i) If a Constituent fails to make payment of consideration to the member in respect
           of any securities purchased by him before the pay-in date notified by the Exchange
           from time to time, then the member shall be entitled to retain those securities in a
           separate Constituent account opened by the member in this regard in the manner
           as prescribed by SEBI / the Exchange after date of pay-out.

           Provided that, where the Constituent fulfils his/its funds obligation towards the
           unpaid securities, the member shall transfer such securities to the demat account of
           the Constituent within the period prescribed by SEBI / the Exchange.

           However, if the Constituent fails to fulfil his/its funds obligation towards the
           securities, the member shall liquidate the securities in the market within the period
           prescribed by SEBI or the Exchange from the date of pay-out.

           Profit/loss arising out of such liquidation of the unpaid securities, if any, shall be
           transferred to/adjusted from the account of the defaulter Constituent.

           Provided further that, the member shall under no circumstances retain the unpaid
           securities of the Constituent received in pay-out beyond the period prescribed by
           SEBI / the Exchange or use it for any other purpose except as prescribed by SEBI /
           the Exchange.

           ii) If a Constituent fails to deliver any one or more securities to the pool account of the
           member in respect of the securities sold by him before the pay-in date notified by the
           Exchange from time to time, such un-discharged obligation in relation to delivering
           any one or more securities shall be deemed to have been closed out at the auction
           price or close-out price, and charges in relation thereto may be debited to the member
           in respect of the security for the respective settlement, to the extent traceable to the
           Constituent who has failed to deliver; otherwise at the close out

                                                                                                                          23
BSE Limited                            Capital Market Segment - Regulations

        price on the date of pay-out in respect of the relevant securities, declared by the
        Exchange. The loss, if any, on account of the close out shall be to the account of the
        Constituent.

        Subject to what is stated above, no further claims shall lie between the Constituent
        and the member in this regard.

        Explanation: If for any reason, the schedule of pay-in and pay-out is modified, the
        above provision shall be made applicable reckoning the date of pay-in and/or payout
        as mentioned in the amended/modified schedule.

3.14 MARGIN TRADING FACILITY

           Every member of the Exchange who desires to provide Margin Trading facility to his
           Constituents may be permitted by the Exchange to do so subject to such norms,
           guidelines, eligibility criteria as may be prescribed by SEBI or the Exchange from time
           to time.

           For the purpose of this Regulation `Margin Trading facility' means and refers to the
           facility pursuant to which part of the transaction value due to the Exchange, at the
           time of purchase of securities, shall be paid by the member on behalf of the
           Constituent on Constituent's request, subject to the norms and the guidelines
           prescribed by SEBI or the Exchange from time to time and on such terms and
           conditions as may be mutually agreed between the member and the Constituent.

3.15 SECURITIES LENDING AND BORROWING

3.15.1  Members as authorized by the Exchange shall be entitled to lend and borrow
        securities and otherwise participate in securities borrowing and lending schemes or
        sub-schemes of Clearing Corporation or any other approved intermediaries
        registered by the Securities and Exchange Board of India. The Exchange shall at any
        time in its discretion withdraw approval to a securities borrowing and lending scheme
        or sub-scheme previously approved by it.

3.15.2  All contracts entered into by or with a member under or pursuant to an Approved
        Scheme shall be deemed to have been made subject to the Rules, Bye-laws and
        Regulations of the Exchange.

3.15.3  Unless repugnant to or inconsistent with the context or the provisions of an approved
        scheme, the Rules, Bye-laws and Regulations of the Exchange, the Good/Bad delivery
        Guidelines issued by SEBI and other notices, circulars, rules, regulations, guidelines and
        directions of SEBI and the Exchange shall apply mutatis mutandis to:-

        (a) a contract by a member to lend securities under an approved scheme in the
              same manner as they apply to a contract by a member to sell securities;

        (b) a contract by a member to borrow securities under an approved scheme in the

                                                                                                                      24
BSE Limited  Capital Market Segment - Regulations

3.15.4          same manner as they apply to a contract by a member to purchase securities;

        (c) a contract by a member to return securities previously borrowed by him under
               an approved scheme in the same manner as they apply to a contract by a
               member to sell securities;

        (d) a contract by a member to receive back securities previously lent by him under
               an approved scheme in the same manner as they apply to a contract by a
               member to purchase securities;

        Member and the Constituent shall comply with the requirements laid down by the
        Exchange in relation to various issues such as eligibility criteria, eligible securities, tenure
        of contracts, agreement with Constituents, unique ID, margins and collaterals, lending
        fees, lending price, position limit, shortages & close-out procedure, corporate actions in
        relation to securities lending and borrowing scheme from time to time.

3.15.5  The rights and obligations of members and their Constituents, as prescribed under the
        Rules, Bye-laws and Regulations of the Exchange and securities laws, in relation to a
        bargain/deal carried out on the Trading platform of BSE shall apply mutatis mutandis
        in relation to contract between, and the rights and obligations of, members and their
        Constituents in relation to lending, borrowing and other transactions under an
        approved scheme unless a contrary is provided thereto.

3.15.6  A breach by a member of the provisions of an Approved Scheme or of any rules,
        procedures, notices, circulars, directions, guidelines or provisions issued or made by the
        Exchange shall be deemed to be a violation by the member of the Rules, Byelaws and
        Regulations of the Exchange or of rules, procedures, notices, circulars, directions,
        guidelines or provisions issued or made by the Exchange, and the Exchange shall be
        entitled to take proceedings and action against the member accordingly including
        suspension of his membership rights, expulsion or declaration of default.

3.15.7  All claims (whether admitted or not), disputes and differences between a member and
        a Constituent in relation to securities lending and borrowing scheme shall
        resolved/adjudicated as per conciliation/arbitration mechanism prescribed in the
        Rules, Bye-laws and Regulations of the Exchange.

3.16 COLLECTION OF SECURITIES TRANSACTION TAX & SEBI TURNOVERFEES

          Every member shall remit to the Exchange the Securities Transaction Tax (STT) and SEBI
          Turnover Fees, in respect of the transactions entered into by him on the Exchange either
          on his own behalf or on behalf of his Constituents in accordance with the procedures
          prescribed by the Relevant Authority from time to time for the calculation and collection
          of such tax and/or fees. Any member who fails to make the payment in accordance with
          the procedures prescribed by the Relevant Authority from time to time would be liable
          for such consequences of non-payment, including but not limited to withdrawal of
          trading facility, appropriation from the monies of the member lying with the Exchange,
          withholding of pay-outs etc., as may be prescribed from time to time.

                                                                                                                          25
BSE Limited                                Capital Market Segment - Regulations

3.17 COLLECTION OF PERMANENT ACCOUNT NUMBER (PAN)

           The members shall -

           (a) collect copies of Permanent Account Number (PAN) cards issued to their existing
                  as well as new clients by the Income-tax department and maintain the same in
                  their record after verifying with the original;

           (b) cross check the aforesaid details collected from their clients with the details on
                  the website of the Income tax department; and

           (c) upload the details of PAN so collected to the Exchanges as part of the unique
                  client code.

Provided that nothing contained in this regulation shall apply in case the client's
details are taken by the member from the database of a KYC Registration Agency,
registered with SEBI, under the SEBI {KYC (Know Your Client) Registration Agency}
Regulations, 2011 or any amendment thereof.

3.18 EMPANELED REFINERIES FOR EGR SEGMENT

(a) For the purpose of EGR Segment, the Exchange may empanel refineries in the
    manner specified by the Exchange from time to time and Electronic Gold Receipts
    pertaining to underlying gold of such empaneled refineries will be available for
    trading in the EGR Segment as determined by the Exchange.

(b) The decision of the Exchange with regard to empanelment of a refinery shall be
    final and binding.

(c) The Exchange, from time to time, will determine the Electronic Gold Receipts
    which may be available for trading in the EGR Segment and the trading in such
    permitted Electronic Gold Receipts shall be subject to further directions by the
    Exchange.

(d) An intending refinery shall make the requisite application to the Exchange and
    comply with all the requirements including payment of fees as prescribed by the
    Exchange from time to time for the purpose of empanelment.

(e) An empaneled refinery is duty bound to comply with the
    circular/notices/directions issued by the Exchange from time to time and shall
    take steps for redressal of grievances of the constituent.

(f) An empaneled refinery shall at all times comply with various requirements
    prescribed by the Exchange including specification of the underlying gold,
    submission of documents on periodical basis or on event basis, manner of
    sourcing the underlying gold and shall provide information/documents as sought

                                           26
BSE Limited  Capital Market Segment - Regulations

    by the Exchange from time to time.

(g) In case of deviation in the fineness or weight of the underlying gold in relation to
    concerned Electronic Gold Receipts, the relevant refinery shall promptly comply
    with the direction(s) issued by the Exchange, failing which, the Exchange shall be
    entitled to take suitable action(s) against such refinery. The Exchange shall not be
    liable to such refinery for any action taken against it in any manner whatsoever.

(h) A constituent who had purchased Electronic Gold Receipts pursuant to a trade
    executed on the platform of the Exchange, agrees that he shall be entitled to receive
    an amount from the concerned refinery as determined in accordance with the
    framework prescribed by the Exchange in case of deviation in the fineness or weight
    of the underlying gold in relation to concerned Electronic Gold Receipts traded by
    him and that no claims, whatsoever, shall lie against the Exchange.

(i) A constituent dealing in Electronic Gold Receipts understands that the Exchange
    would entertain only such grievances as permitted in terms of the framework
    prescribed by SEBI/the Exchange from time to time.

3.19 SPOT PRICE POLLING FOR EGR SEGMENT

         (a) The Exchange may adopt spot price polling mechanism for the purpose of
              arriving at a spot price for a particular Electronic Gold Receipt in accordance
              with guidelines and directions provided by SEBI.

(b) The Exchange shall have a documented policy for the spot price polling
    mechanism.

(c) The Exchange shall display the spot price polling mechanism adopted for every
    Electronic Gold Receipt on its website.

(d) The Exchange shall assign a code such as X1, X2, X3...etc. for polling participants
    of a particular Electronic Gold Receipt and reveal their location and price (s) for
    the day.

(e) This information shall be updated on Exchange's website every day for every
    Electronic Gold Receipt traded on the Exchange's platform.

(f) The Exchange shall endeavour in increasing the sample size used for fixing the
    daily spot prices.

(g) The Exchange shall review its spot price polling participants at least once a
    month to identify participants habitually polling unrealistic prices.

                                                                                                              27
BSE Limited  Capital Market Segment - Regulations

(h) These participants may be put under watch and subsequently removed from
    the panel if such instances reoccur despite appropriate communications.

(i) The Exchange shall provide a separate feedback window for receiving
    complaints from participants, constituents and others regarding spot price
    polling.

(j) The Exchange shall address such complaints in a time-bound manner. Further,
    the Exchange shall keep the audit trail of all such complaints received and the
    steps taken for redressal thereto.

                                                                                     28
BSE Limited                      Capital Market Segment - Regulations

             CHAPTER 4 - CONDUCT OF BUSINESS BY MEMBERS

4.1    OFFICE RELATED PROCEDURE
4.1.1
       No member shall carry on in any office or premises any activity connected with or
       pertaining to its operations as a member of the Exchange unless it has intimated to
       the Exchange the full address of such office or premises with details of the activity
       proposed to be so carried on therein and the person in whose supervision and control
       it is to be so carried on.

4.1.2  Each department for trading, shall be under the supervision and control of the
       member establishing it and of the personnel delegated with such authority and
       responsibility in this regard.

4.1.3  Every member shall ensure that all employees and persons acting on his behalf on the
       Trading System shall subscribe at all times to high standards of professional expertise
       and integrity.

4.1.4  Each member shall at all times maintain such infrastructure, staff, communication
       facilities and records so as to be able to service his Constituents satisfactorily and as
       per the requirements enumerated in the Bye-laws, Rules and Regulations of the
       Exchange or Securities Laws.

4.1.5  Where the Exchange feels it necessary, in the public interest to do so, it may at its own
       instance or on a complaint from another member or client, seek explanation from the
       member regarding the level of service or professional conduct of the member or any
       of his staff where such service or conduct has been found unsatisfactory or contrary
       to principles enumerated in the Bye-laws, Rules and Regulations of the Exchange, or
       notifications, directions or circulars issued there under.

4.2    SUPERVISION

4.2.1 PROCEDURES TO BE FOLLOWED

       (a) Each member shall establish, maintain, and enforce procedures to supervise its
              business and to supervise the activities of its employees that are reasonably
              designed to achieve compliance with the Byelaws, Rules and Regulations of the
              Exchange and any notifications, directions, etc. issued there under as well as the
              relevant statutory Acts, Rules & Regulations.

       (b) The member, for the purpose of better control may, if so required, maintain an
                                                                                                                     29
BSE Limited  Capital Market Segment - Regulations

             internal record of the names of all persons who are designated as supervisory
             personnel and the dates for which such designation is or was effective.

(c) Every member shall specifically authorise in writing, a person or persons who
      may be authorised to transact on behalf of the member and to do such acts
      which the member may wish to delegate to such person, and make available a
      copy of such power of attorney to the Exchange before such person transacts
      any business on the Exchange.

(d) A member shall maintain such records and make available for inspection by any
      person authorised in this behalf by the Exchange, such information related to
      such member, including the member's financial condition, as prescribed by the
      Exchange .

(e) The member shall pay such fees, charges, liabilities, annual subscription and
      other sum as the Exchange may notify from time to time, within such time and
      manner as required by the Exchange.

(f) The member must inform the Exchange of any change in the status,
      constitution, operation and activities of the member, which change shall be
      effected only subject to receipt of such approvals and/or following such
      procedure as may be specified by the Exchange.

4.2.2 INTERNAL INSPECTIONS

           Each member shall endeavor to conduct a review, of its operations, systems and
           processes, annually which shall be reasonably designed to assist in detecting and
           preventing violations of and achieving compliance with Securities and Exchange
           Board of India Act, 1992 and Regulations framed thereunder, the Securities Contracts
           (Regulation) Act, 1956 and Rules framed thereunder and Bye-laws, Rules and
           Regulations of the Exchange and Circulars issued by SEBI, and the Exchange.

4.2.3 INTERNAL AUDIT

           Each member shall conduct a complete internal audit at such periodicity, by such
           professionals and having such scope as may be prescribed by the Exchange/SEBI from
           time to time.

Each member shall comply with the requirements of the system/algorithmic audit or
such other audit as may be prescribed by the SEBI/Exchange from time to time.

4.2.4 WRITTEN APPROVAL
                                                                                                                          30
BSE Limited                     Capital Market Segment - Regulations

       Each member shall establish procedures for review and endorsement by an
       appropriate senior officer in writing, of the record of all transactions and
       correspondence of its employees, pertaining to solicitation or execution of any
       securities transaction.

4.2.5 QUALIFICATIONS INVESTIGATED

          Each member shall have the responsibility and duty to ascertain the good character,
          business repute, qualifications, and experience of any person prior to certifying as
          such in the application for registration of such person, with the Exchange, as an
          Approved User, or in any other capacity.

4.3 RELATION WITH CONSTITUENTS

4.3.1  At the time of establishing a relationship with a new Constituent, the member must
       take reasonable steps to assess the background, genuineness, beneficial identity,
4.3.2  financial soundness of such person, and his investment objectives by obtaining all
4.3.3  necessary documents, including account opening documents executed by such
4.3.4  Constituent, as may be prescribed by the SEBI/Exchange from time to time. The
       member shall also obtain from all Constituents, other than individual Constituents, an
       authenticated copy of the board resolution/authority letter permitting trading on the
       Exchange.

       Without prejudice to the generality of regulation 4.3.1, the member shall perform
       such other specific verification of the Constituent as may be deemed necessary by him
       or as may be required by SEBI and/or the Exchange or in terms of any law for the time
       being in force.

       The member shall, before registering a Constituent, furnish him with an account
       opening form, which shall be in such format, shall be accompanied by such
       documents, including segregation into mandatory and non-mandatory documents,
       and shall have such further and other requirements, as may be specified by SEBI
       and/or the Exchange.

       Notwithstanding anything contained in Regulations 4.3.2 & 4.3.3, a member may use
       the Constituent's Know Your Client (KYC) information which is available with a KYC
       Registration Agency registered with SEBI under the Securities and Exchange Board of
       India {KYC (Know Your Client) Registration Agency} Regulations, 2011, subject to such
       conditions and restrictions as may be specified by SEBI or the Exchange from time to
       time.

4.3.5  Every member shall enter into an agreement with each of his Authorised Persons,
       before accepting or placing orders on behalf of the Constituents of the Authorised
       Person. Such agreement shall include provisions specified by the Exchange in this
       behalf and shall be in such format as may be prescribed by the Exchange from time

                                31
BSE Limited  Capital Market Segment - Regulations

       to time. However, the member's responsibility shall not in any way be reduced due to
       non-execution of agreement with the Authorised Person.

4.3.6  The member shall provide to the Exchange and/or SEBI, the information relating to
       Constituent's dealings and balances of funds, including margins, in such manner and
       at such periodicity as may be specified by SEBI and/or the Exchange.

4.3.7  The member who maintain their own websites, shall display the set of standard
       documents required in connection with Constituent registration and may allow secure
       online access to their Constituents to such trading and settlement related information
       pertaining to them in such manner as may be specified by SEBI and/or the Exchange.

4.3.8  A member shall evolve policies and procedures for such matters pertaining to dealings
       with the Constituent and the treatment of the Constituent, as may be specified by
       SEBI and/or the Exchange and shall disclose the same to the Constituent or display the
       same in such manner as may be specified.

4.3.9  A member shall make his Constituent aware of trading segment to which the member
       is admitted, particulars of SEBI registration number, employee primarily responsible
       for the Constituent's affairs, the precise nature of the member's liability for the
       business to be conducted, the risk associated with the business in securities trading,
       including any limitations on that liability and the capacity in which the member acts
       and the Constituent's liability by reason of dealing in securities, by issuing to the
       Constituent a copy of the model Risk Disclosure Document which shall be in such
       format as may be prescribed by SEBI/the Exchange from time to time. The member
       shall furnish a copy of the Risk Disclosure Document to all his clients.

4.3.10 A member shall provide to his/its Constituents (at no extra costs) extracts of relevant
          provisions governing the rights and obligations of Constituents as may be specified
          either in the Bye-laws, Rules and Regulations, or the relevant manuals, notifications,
          circulars of the Exchange/SEBI and any additions or amendments thereto or of any
          regulatory authority, to the extent it governs the relationship between the member
          and its/his Constituents.

4.3.11 A member may also bring to the notice of his Constituents any indictments, penalties,
          etc. imposed on him by the Exchange or any other regulatory authority.

4.3.12 A member may obtain a power of attorney from his Constituents only in strict
          compliance with the directions, guidelines or Circulars of SEBI and/or the Exchange,
          issued from time to time.

             32
BSE Limited  Capital Market Segment - Regulations

4.3.13 Further responsibilities of members:

          (a) A member shall make adequate disclosure of the relevant material information
                  of his dealings with his Constituents.

          (b) No member or person associated with the member shall guarantee a
                  Constituent against a loss in any transactions effected by the member for such
                  Constituent.

4.4 DISCLOSURE OF INVESTOR COMPLAINTS, ARBITRATION DETAILS AND DISCIPLINARY
          ACTION

          Unless otherwise directed by SEBI or other competent authority, court or tribunal, the
          Exchange may disclose the details of the complaints lodged by Constituents/ investors
          against the members on its website or on any other website designated for this
          purpose, including the details pertaining to arbitration and penal action taken by the
          Exchange or SEBI against the members, in such format and update the same at such
          periodicity as may be specified by SEBI.

4.5    GUIDELINES GOVERNING RELATIONSHIP BETWEEN MEMBER AND CONSTITUENT
4.5.1
       A member shall not recommend to a Constituent sale or purchase of securities traded
       on the Trading System, unless he has reasonable grounds to believe that such
       recommendation is suitable for the Constituent on the basis of the facts, if any,
       disclosed by the Constituent, whether in writing or orally, regarding the objectives,
       Constituent's security holdings, financial soundness and investment objectives.

4.5.2  A member shall make adequate disclosure of the relevant material information of his
       dealing with his Constituents, including the current best price of trade and trade or
       order quantities on the Trading System, as also any allocation policy, inter se, between
       the Constituents, any relevant announcement from the Exchange relating to margin,
       trading restrictions as to price, quantity or where the member is a counter party to a
       trade executed on the Trading System with the Constituent.

4.5.3  Where a member manages a discretionary account for or on behalf of a Constituent,
       he should abide by the Securities and Exchange Board of India (Portfolio Managers)
       Rules and Regulations, 1993 and any circulars/directives issued by SEBI from time to
       time in this regard.

4.5.4  A member shall not furnish any false or misleading information or advice to induce
                                                                                                                    33
BSE Limited  Capital Market Segment - Regulations

4.5.5   the Constituent to do a business in particular securities and which will enable the
        member to make unfair gains.

        A member shall explain the Trading System and the order matching process to the
        Constituent before accepting any orders from him.

4.5.6   A member shall inform his Constituents whether he does client based trading or
        Pro-account! Own Account trading. The member shall disclose the aforesaid
        information-

        (i) to his new Constituents upfront at the time of first establishing the relationship
              with them;

        (ii) to the Constituents before carrying out any Pro-account trading, in case he
              presently does not do Pro-account ! Own Account trading, but chooses to do so
              at a later date.

4.5.7   Where a Constituent requires an order to be placed or any of his orders to be
4.5.8   modified after the order has entered the Trading System but has not been executed,
        the member may obtain order placement ! modification details in writing from the
        Constituent. The member shall accordingly provide to the Constituent the relevant
        order confirmation ! modification slip or copy thereof, forthwith, if so required by the
        Constituent.

        Where the Constituent requires any of his orders to be cancelled after the order has
        been entered in the Trading System but has not been executed, the member may,
        obtain order cancellation details from the Constituent. The member shall provide to
        the Constituent the relevant order cancellation details, forthwith, if so required by
        the Constituent.

4.5.9   A member may, if he/it so desires, obtain in writing, the delivery and payment
        requirements in any instructions that it receives from the Constituent. Where a
        member receives a request for order modification or order cancellation from the
        Constituent, it shall duly bring it to its notice that if the order results in a trade in the
        meantime, the request for modification or cancellation cannot be executed.

4.5.10  A member shall act promptly in accordance with the instructions provided by the
        Constituent unless he has a discretion as to the time of execution of such instructions,
        in which case it must exercise its judgment as to the best moment for execution of
        such instruction in the interest of the Constituent.

4.5.11  Where a member is required to pay margin money on transaction executed on behalf
        of his Constituent, he shall collect the same from the Constituent in such form and

                                                                                                                      34
BSE Limited                                          Capital Market Segment - Regulations

        manner as may be specified by the Exchange.

4.5.12  Where a Constituent requires an executed trade to be cancelled, the member shall
        obtain a written request for trade cancellation from the Constituent. In all instances
        of trade cancellation requests, the member shall explain to the Constituent that the
        right to approve or reject such trade cancellation request rests with the Exchange.

4.5.13  i) A member shall make pay out of the funds to the Constituent or deliver securities to
        the demat account of the Constituent, as the case may be, to the Constituent within
        one (1) working day of receipt of the payout from the Exchange if the Constituent has
        fulfilled its pay-in obligation.

        ii) In case, the Constituent fails to make the payment for the securities purchased by
        it or make the delivery of securities, to the member before the pay - in day, then the
        member shall deal with the securities (which are unpaid or undelivered) in the manner
        prescribed by SEBI / the Exchange from time to time.

        iii) If the Constituent fulfils his/its funds obligation towards the securities lying in the
        separate Constituent account maintained by the member, the member shall transfer
        such securities to the demat account of the Constituent within the period prescribed
        by SEBI / the Exchange. However, if the Constituent fails to fulfil his/its funds
        obligation towards such securities, the member shall liquidate the securities in the
        market within the period prescribed by SEBI / the Exchange from the date of pay-out.
        The unpaid securities shall be closed out from the Unique Client Code (UCC) of the
        Constituent.

        Profit/loss arising out of such liquidation of the unpaid securities, if any, shall be
        transferred to/adjusted from the account of the defaulter Constituent.

        Provided further that, the member shall under no circumstances retain the securities
        of the Constituent received in pay-out beyond the period prescribed by the relevant
        authority or use it for any other purpose except as prescribed by the relevant
        authority.

4.5.14  A member may maintain a running account, if specifically so authorised by the
        Constituent, subject to such conditions as may be specified by SEBI and/or the
        Exchange from time to time.

4.5.15 (a) A member shall neither make nor receive payment in cash from his
               Constituents.

        (b) A member shall make or receive all payments to/from his Constituents only in
             the manner prescribed by the SEBI/the Exchange from time to time.

                                                                                          35
BSE Limited                          Capital Market Segment - Regulations

        (c) Similarly, in the case of securities also, giving/taking delivery of securities in
              `demat mode' should be directly to/from the `beneficiary accounts' of the
              Constituents except delivery of securities to a different account, as may be
              directed by SEBI and/or the Exchange for unpaid securities or to a recognised
              entity under the approved scheme of the Exchange and /or SEBI.

              Provided that the unpaid securities shall be dealt with in such manner as may
              be provided by SEBI and/or the Exchange from time to time.

              The member shall not deal with the collateral securities of the Constituent in
              any manner which is contrary to the direction of SEBI and/or the relevant
              authority, from time to time, including creating pledge thereof in favour of any
              banks/non-banking financial companies in order to borrow funds for the
              purpose of meeting the shortfall of margin required to be deposited by the
              Constituent of such member. The securities deposited by the Constituent as
              collateral shall be utilized by the member solely for the purpose of meeting the
              margin requirements of the Constituent depositing such collateral.

4.5.16  Where a Power of Attorney is executed by the Constituent in favour of the member,
        authorising the member to operate the Constituent's bank and demat accounts, such
        Power of Attorney shall be subject to such terms and conditions as may be prescribed
        by the Exchange from time to time.

4.6     CODE OF CONDUCT FOR MEMBERS

4.6.1 Adherence to SEBI Code of Conduct

           A member shall at all times adhere to the Code of Conduct as specified by the
           Securities and Exchange Board of India (Stock Brokers and Sub-Brokers) Regulations,
           1992.

4.6.2 General Principles-

           (a) Professionalism: A member, in the conduct of his business, shall observe high
                  standards of just and equitable principles of trade.

        (b) Adherence to Trading Practices: A member shall adhere to the Rules,
              Regulations and Bye-laws of the Exchange and shall comply with such
              operational parameters, circulars, rulings, notices, guidelines and instructions of
              the Relevant Authority as may be applicable from time to time.

        (c) Honesty and Fairness: In conducting his business activities, a member shall act
              honestly and fairly, in the best interest of his Constituents.

                                     36
BSE Limited  Capital Market Segment - Regulations

          (d) Capabilities: A member shall have and employ the resources and procedures
                  which are needed for the proper performance of his business activities.

4.6.3 Trading Principles

          (a) The members shall ensure that the fiduciary and other obligations imposed on
                  them and their staff by the various statutory acts, rules and regulations is
                  complied with.

          (b) The member shall ensure -

                  (i) that the employee of the member, who commits the member to a
                         transaction has the necessary authority to do so.

                  (ii) that his/its employees are adequately trained in operating in the relevant
                         market segment in which they deal, are aware of their own, and their
                         organization's responsibilities as well as the relevant statutory acts
                         governing the member, the Rules, Regulations and Bye-laws of the
                         Exchange, including any additions or amendments thereof.

          (c) A member shall be responsible for all the actions, including for the trades
                  originating through or with the use of all following variables member Id and User
                  Id, at that point of time. However, if the member satisfies the Exchange that the
                  action(s) and/or trade(s) took place due to fraud or misrepresentation by any
                  other person other than his authorised person(s) and/or that the action(s)
                  and/or trades did not originate from any of his Approved Workstations, the
                  Exchange may issue such directions as it considers just and reasonable. The
                  directions may include referring the matter to arbitration/ Relevant Authority
                  and /or annulment of trade(s) so effected.

          (d) No member or person associated with a member shall make improper use of
                  the Constituent's securities or funds provided as a collateral. Such securities or
                  funds shall be used only for satisfying the obligations of such Constituent in the
                  manner specified by SEBI / the Exchange from time to time.

          (e) No member shall publish and circulate or cause to be published or circulated, any
                  notice, circular, advertisement, newspaper article, investment service or
                  communication of any kind which purports to report any transaction as a
                  purchase or sale of any security, unless such member can establish, if called
                  upon, that such transaction was a bona-fide purchase or sale of such security; or
                  which purports to quote the purchase/sale price for any security unless such

                                                                                                                          37
BSE Limited  Capital Market Segment - Regulations

             member can establish, if called upon, that such quotation represents a bona-
             fide order of such security.

       (f) When entering into or arranging transactions, the members must at all times
             ensure that great care is taken not to misrepresent in any way, the nature of the
             transaction.

       (g) No member shall exercise any discretionary power in a Constituent's Account,
             unless such Constituent has given a prior written authorisation to such member
